    def test_no_free_string_enum_value_bypass_across_modules() -> None:
        member_values: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        for path in iter_python_files(SRC_ROOT):
            for value, owners in enum_member_values(parse(path), path).items():
                member_values.setdefault(value, []).extend(owners)
        offenders: list[str] = []
        for path in iter_python_files(SRC_ROOT):
            tree = parse(path)
            module_owners = {
                value
                for value, owners in member_values.items()
                if any(str(path) in owner for owner in owners)
            }
            for literal in string_literals(tree):
                if literal in PRESENTATION_OR_PATH_STRINGS:
                    continue
                if literal not in member_values:
                    continue
                if literal in module_owners:
                    continue
                owners = member_values[literal]

                offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)}:{literal!r} (owned by {owners_text})")

_____________ test_no_public_production_symbol_used_only_by_tests ______________

    def test_no_public_production_symbol_used_only_by_tests() -> None:
        offenders: list[str] = []
        for path in iter_python_files(SRC_ROOT):
            if path.name == "__init__.py":
                continue
            for symbol in public_top_level_symbols(parse(path)):
                used_in_src = referenced_in(SRC_ROOT, symbol, path)
                used_in_tests = referenced_in(TESTS_ROOT, symbol, path)
                if used_in_tests and not used_in_src:
                    offenders.append(f"{path.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)}:{symbol}")
>       assert not offenders, f"Production symbols referenced only from tests: {offenders}"
